    41	// ---- CAS base stamps -------------------------------------------------------
    42	
    43	// sha256 over content with line endings normalized — a delta authored on Windows
    44	// must fingerprint identically to the same store checked out with LF
    45	function fingerprint(text) {
    46	  const norm = text.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').replace(/\r/g, '\n');
    47	  return 'sha256:' + crypto.createHash('sha256').update(norm, 'utf8').digest('hex');
    48	}
    49	
    50	const STAMP_LINE_RE = /^<!--\s*apriori-base:\s*(\S+)\s*-->\s*$/gm;
    51	// any standalone comment line ATTEMPTING to be a stamp — a malformed attempt must be an error,
    52	// never silently treated as "unstamped" (that would quietly disable CAS)
    53	const STAMP_ATTEMPT_RE = /^\s*<!--(?=[^>]*apriori-base)[^>]*-->\s*$/gm;
    54	
    55	// → { stamp: 'sha256:<hex>' | 'new' | null, problems: [] }
    56	// Exactly one stamp line, before the first delta section, well-formed digest.
    57	function parseStamp(text) {
    58	  const problems = [];
    59	  const sectionIdx = text.search(SECTION_SPLIT_RE);
    60	  STAMP_ATTEMPT_RE.lastIndex = 0;
    61	  let m;
    62	  while ((m = STAMP_ATTEMPT_RE.exec(text)) !== null) {
    63	    STAMP_LINE_RE.lastIndex = 0;
    64	    if (!STAMP_LINE_RE.test(m[0].trim())) problems.push(`malformed apriori-base stamp line: ${m[0].trim()}`);
    65	  }
    66	  const found = [];
    67	  STAMP_LINE_RE.lastIndex = 0;
    68	  while ((m = STAMP_LINE_RE.exec(text)) !== null) found.push({ value: m[1], index: m.index });
    69	  if (!found.length) return { stamp: null, problems };
    70	  if (found.length > 1) problems.push('carries more than one apriori-base stamp line');
    71	  for (const f of found) {
    72	    if (sectionIdx >= 0 && f.index > sectionIdx) problems.push('apriori-base stamp appears after the first delta section heading');
    73	    if (f.value !== 'new' && !/^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(f.value)) problems.push(`malformed apriori-base stamp '${f.value}'`);
    74	  }
    75	  return { stamp: problems.length ? null : found[0].value, problems };
    76	}
    77	
    78	// ---- delta parsing ---------------------------------------------------------
    79	
    80	// per-line forms of the shared regexes (no /m — the walker feeds one normalized line at a time)
    81	const SECTION_LINE_RE = /^##\s+(ADDED|MODIFIED|REMOVED|RENAMED)\s+Requirements\s*$/;
    82	const NOTES_LINE_RE = /^##\s+Notes\s*$/;   // the one h2 the parser is allowed to ignore
    83	const H2_LINE_RE = /^##\s/;
    84	const REQ_LINE_RE = /^###\s+Requirement:\s+(.+?)\s*$/;
    85	const SCENARIO_LINE_RE = /^####\s+Scenario:/;
    86	const RENAME_LINE_RE = /^\s*-\s*(.+?)\s*->\s*(.+?)\s*$/;
    87	const STAMP_ATTEMPT_LINE_RE = /^\s*<!--(?=[^>]*apriori-base)[^>]*-->\s*$/;
    88	const STAMP_STRICT_LINE_RE = /^<!--\s*apriori-base:\s*(\S+)\s*-->\s*$/;
    89	const FENCE_LINE_RE = /^\s*```/;
    90	
    91	// strict form: a sequential, fully-consuming line walker — every line (outside
    92	// code fences, which are opaque) belongs to exactly one legal construct, and
    93	// anything else is a problems[] entry carrying its 1-based line number.
    94	// Well-formed deltas parse byte-identically to the old split()-based grammar.
    95	function parseDeltaStrict(text) {
    96	  const problems = [];
    97	  const buckets = { ADDED: new Map(), MODIFIED: new Map(), REMOVED: new Map(), RENAMED: [] };
    98	  const seen = new Map();               // name -> first section carrying it
    99	  const lines = text.split('\n').map((l) => (l.endsWith('\r') ? l.slice(0, -1) : l));
   100	
   101	  let state = 'FILE_PREAMBLE';          // | 'IN_SECTION' | 'IN_REQUIREMENT' | 'SKIP_UNRECOGNIZED' | 'IN_NOTES'
   102	  let kind = null;                      // current section kind while IN_SECTION/IN_REQUIREMENT
   103	  let blockName = null, blockLines = null, blockDiscard = false;
   104	  let inFence = false;
   105	  let stamp = null, stampSeen = false, stampProblems = false, sectionSeen = false;
   106	
   107	  const flush = () => {
   108	    if (blockLines === null) return;
   109	    if (!blockDiscard) buckets[kind].set(blockName, blockLines.join('\n').replace(/\s+$/, '') + '\n');
   110	    blockName = null; blockLines = null; blockDiscard = false;
   111	  };
   112	  const stampProblem = (msg) => { problems.push(msg); stampProblems = true; };
   113	
   114	  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
   115	    const line = lines[i], n = i + 1;
   116	    if (inFence) {                      // opaque: never structure, never a problem
   117	      if (blockLines !== null) blockLines.push(line);
   118	      if (FENCE_LINE_RE.test(line)) inFence = false;
   119	      continue;
   120	    }
   240	    const renamedBlock = block.replace(/^###\s+Requirement:\s+.+$/m, `### Requirement: ${newName}`);
   241	    // rebuild the map preserving order, swapping the key at the old position
   242	    const rebuilt = new Map();
   243	    for (const [k, v] of store) rebuilt.set(k === oldName ? newName : k, k === oldName ? renamedBlock : v);
   244	    store.clear();
   245	    for (const [k, v] of rebuilt) store.set(k, v);
   246	    renamed.push(`${oldName} -> ${newName}`);
   247	    renamedTargets.add(newName);
   248	  }
   249	  for (const [name, block] of delta.ADDED) {
   250	    if (store.has(name)) {
   251	      // idempotent rerun: identical content means this delta already merged — a no-op, not a collision.
   252	      // But a name just created by THIS run's RENAMED is a same-delta collision, never a rerun.
   253	      if (!renamedTargets.has(name) && store.get(name).trim() === block.trim()) unchanged.push(name);
   254	      else conflicts.push(`ADDED '${name}' already exists in store`);
   255	    } else { store.set(name, block); merged.push(name); }
   256	  }
   257	  for (const [name, block] of delta.MODIFIED) {
   258	    if (!store.has(name)) conflicts.push(`MODIFIED '${name}' has no target in store`);
   259	    // already applied (rerun signature) — same trim comparison the ADDED no-op uses
   260	    else if (store.get(name).trim() === block.trim()) unchanged.push(name);
   261	    else { store.set(name, block); modified.push(name); }
   262	  }
   263	  for (const name of delta.REMOVED.keys()) {
   264	    if (!store.has(name)) {
   265	      // rerun signature: already deprecated BY THIS CHANGE → no-op; by another change → real collision
   266	      const depByThis = `${name}  _deprecated (superseded by ${change})_`;
   267	      if (store.has(depByThis)) { unchanged.push(`${name} (already deprecated)`); continue; }
   268	      const depOther = [...store.keys()].find((k) => k.startsWith(`${name}  _deprecated (superseded by `));
   269	      if (depOther) { conflicts.push(`REMOVED '${name}' was already deprecated by a different change ('${depOther}')`); continue; }
   270	      conflicts.push(`REMOVED '${name}' has no target in store`);
   271	    } else {
   272	      const block = store.get(name);
   273	      const nl = block.indexOf('\n');
   274	      store.set(name, block.slice(0, nl) + `  _deprecated (superseded by ${change})_` + block.slice(nl));
   275	      deprecated.push(name);
   276	    }
   277	  }
   278	  return { store, merged, modified, deprecated, renamed, conflicts, unchanged };
   279	}
   280	
   281	function renderStore(storeText, store) {
   282	  const idx = storeText.indexOf('### Requirement:');
   283	  const header = idx >= 0 ? storeText.slice(0, idx) : '';
   284	  return header + [...store.values()].join('\n');
   285	}
   286	
   287	// ---- paths and discovery ---------------------------------------------------
   288	
   289	// Realpath containment: target (followed through symlinks; not-yet-existing targets judged
   290	// by their nearest existing ancestor) must resolve STRICTLY inside root. Fail-closed.
   291	function containsReal(root, target) {
   292	  let realRoot;
   293	  try { realRoot = fs.realpathSync(root); } catch { return false; }
   294	  let cur = path.resolve(target);
   295	  const rest = [];
   296	  while (!fs.existsSync(cur)) {
   297	    const parent = path.dirname(cur);
   298	    if (parent === cur) return false;
   299	    rest.unshift(path.basename(cur));
   300	    cur = parent;
   301	  }
   302	  let realCur;
   303	  try { realCur = fs.realpathSync(cur); } catch { return false; }
   304	  const full = rest.length ? path.join(realCur, ...rest) : realCur;
   305	  return full !== realRoot && (full + path.sep).startsWith(realRoot + path.sep);
   306	}
   307	
   308	function mdFilesUnder(dir) {
   309	  const out = [];
   310	  const walk = (d) => {
   311	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   312	      const p = path.join(d, e.name);
   313	      // Dirents reflect lstat: a symlink is neither file nor directory. Follow it explicitly —
   314	      // silently skipping a symlinked dir would hide deltas (fail-open); containment judges the target.
   315	      let isDir = e.isDirectory();
   316	      if (e.isSymbolicLink()) {
   317	        try { isDir = fs.statSync(p).isDirectory(); } catch { isDir = false; /* broken link: fall through, read fails loudly */ }
   318	      }
   319	      if (isDir) walk(p);
   320	      else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) out.push(p);
   321	    }
   322	  };
   323	  if (fs.existsSync(dir)) walk(dir);
   324	  return out.sort();
   325	}
   326	
   327	// Discover a change's delta files. → { files: [{abs, suffix}], errors: [] } — errors are
   328	// VALIDATION class (exit 2 on both surfaces): bad name, missing dir, escape, zero files.
   329	function discoverDeltas(changesDirAbs, name) {
   330	  const errors = [];
   620	    while ((m = re.exec(stripped)) !== null) titles.push(m[1]);
   621	  };
   622	  for (const list of modifiedBlocks.values()) for (const { oldBlock, newBlock } of list) { collect(oldBlock); collect(newBlock); }
   623	  const res = matcher.batch(titles);
   624	  if (res.failure) { warn('matcher failure: ' + res.failure); return; }
   625	  const titleId = new Map();
   626	  titles.forEach((t, i) => { if (!titleId.has(t)) titleId.set(t, res.ids[i]); });
   627	  const idOf = (t) => { const v = titleId.get(t); return v === undefined ? null : v; };
   628	  const entries = [];
   629	  for (const [file, list] of modifiedBlocks)
   630	    for (const { name, oldBlock, newBlock } of list)
   631	      entries.push({ file, name, ...compareModifiedBlock(oldBlock, newBlock, idOf) });
   632	  entries.sort((a, b) => (a.file === b.file ? (a.name < b.name ? -1 : 1) : (a.file < b.file ? -1 : 1)));
   633	  const seg = formatIntegrityHuman(entries);
   634	  if (seg) out.push('\n' + seg);
   635	}
   636	
   637	// ---- archive commit machinery ----------------------------------------------
   638	
   639	// colon-free date-time stamp YYYY-MM-DDThhmm (CLI's own clock; never an agent guess)
   640	function archiveStamp(d) {
   641	  const p = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
   642	  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${p(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${p(d.getDate())}T${p(d.getHours())}${p(d.getMinutes())}`;
   643	}
   644	
   645	const CHANGE_NAME_RE = /^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$/;   // bare kebab-case — same rule as `apriori new`
   646	
   647	
   648	// Move an in-flight change dir (bare name) → changes/archive/<stamp>-<name>/
   649	// Fail-closed: source AND destination must resolve (realpath) strictly inside changesDir.
   650	function archiveChangeDir(changesDir, name, now, ops) {
   651	  const rename = (ops && ops.renameSync) || fs.renameSync;
   652	  if (!CHANGE_NAME_RE.test(name)) throw new Error(`invalid change name '${name}' — bare kebab-case only`);
   653	  const src = path.join(changesDir, name);
   654	  if (!fs.existsSync(src)) return null;
   655	  if (!containsReal(changesDir, src)) throw new Error(`change path escapes ${changesDir}`);
   656	  const dest = path.join(changesDir, 'archive', `${archiveStamp(now)}-${name}`);
   657	  if (!containsReal(changesDir, dest)) throw new Error(`archive destination escapes ${changesDir} (symlinked archive/?)`);
   658	  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(dest), { recursive: true });
   659	  rename(src, dest);
   660	  return dest;
   661	}
   662	
   663	const CATEGORY_LABELS = [['merged (ADDED)', 'merged'], ['modified (MODIFIED)', 'modified'],
   664	  ['deprecated (REMOVED)', 'deprecated'], ['renamed (RENAMED)', 'renamed'], ['already merged (no-op)', 'unchanged']];
   665	
   666	// High-level archive: the whole change, four phases (preflight → stage → commit → move).
   667	// → { code, out: [], err: [] } — pure return; cli() prints. ops is the fault-injection seam.
   668	function archiveChange(o) {
   669	  const out = [], err = [];
   670	  const ops = o.ops || { writeFileSync: fs.writeFileSync.bind(fs), renameSync: fs.renameSync.bind(fs), rmSync: fs.rmSync.bind(fs) };
   671	  const cwd = o.cwd || process.cwd();
   672	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, o.changesDir || path.join('apriori', 'changes'));
   673	  const storeRoot = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs');
   674	
   675	  // --- phase 1: preflight (no writes) ---
   676	  const d = discoverDeltas(changesDir, o.change);
   677	  if (d.errors.length) { err.push(...d.errors.map((e) => `archive: ${e}`)); return { code: 2, out, err }; }
   678	  const p = buildProjection(storeRoot, d.files, o.change);
   679	  if (p.validation.length) { err.push(...p.validation.map((e) => `archive: ${e}`)); return { code: 2, out, err }; }
   680	  // per-module report for whatever merged cleanly (dry-run info comes first, then failures)
   681	  for (const suffix of p.modules) {
   682	    const r = p.perModule.get(suffix);
   683	    if (!r || r.conflicts.length) continue;
   684	    out.push(`--- ${suffix}`);
   685	    for (const [label, key] of CATEGORY_LABELS) if (r[key].length) out.push(`${label}: ${r[key].join(', ')}`);
   686	  }
   687	  const casDenials = [];
   688	  if (p.unstampedMutations.length) {
   689	    const w = casWaiver(cwd, o.noCas);
   690	    if (w.problem) {
   691	      casDenials.push(`${w.problem} — a broken config never equals a waiver (denied)`);
   692	    } else if (!w.waiver) {
   693	      for (const sfx of p.unstampedMutations)
   694	        casDenials.push(`${unstampedWarning(sfx)} — denied by default; waive with --no-cas or a | cas | optional | config row`);
   695	    } else {
   696	      for (const sfx of p.unstampedMutations) out.push(`warning: ${unstampedWarning(sfx)}`);
   697	      out.push(`note: cas waived by ${w.waiver}`);
   698	    }
   699	  }
   700	  for (const n of p.notes) out.push(`note: ${n}`);
   701	  // CAS denial joins the preflight failure set — every diagnosis stays visible in one report
   702	  const preflightFailures = [...casDenials, ...p.hygiene, ...p.casMismatches, ...p.conflicts];
   703	  if (preflightFailures.length) {
   704	    for (const f of preflightFailures) err.push(`archive: ${f}`);
   705	    out.push('\nRESULT: FAILED PREFLIGHT — nothing written');
   706	    return { code: 1, out, err };
   707	  }
   708	  const repaired = new Set(p.repaired);                     // already applied — no writes for these
   709	  const jobs = d.files.filter(({ suffix }) => !repaired.has(suffix)).map(({ suffix }) => {
   710	    const store = path.join(storeRoot, ...suffix.split('/'));
   711	    return { suffix, store, tmp: store + '.tmp-archive', text: p.texts.get(suffix) };
   712	  }).sort((a, b) => (a.store < b.store ? -1 : 1));
   713	  if (o.write) {
   714	    for (const j of jobs) if (fs.existsSync(j.tmp)) {
   715	      err.push(`archive: temp file already exists: ${j.tmp} — another run in flight or a manual-recovery artifact; not touching it`);
   716	      out.push('\nRESULT: FAILED PREFLIGHT — nothing written');
   717	      return { code: 1, out, err };
   718	    }
   719	    if (o.changesDirExplicit) {
   720	      // destination containment is checked before any write, not at move time
   721	      const probe = path.join(changesDir, 'archive', 'probe');
   722	      if (!containsReal(changesDir, probe)) {
   723	        err.push(`archive: archive destination escapes ${changesDir} (symlinked archive/?)`);
   724	        return { code: 2, out, err };
   725	      }
   726	    }
   727	  }
   728	  // the modified-block integrity section: AFTER every preflight guard (incl. the --write
   729	  // temp/containment checks above), BEFORE any write-result line — informative only
   730	  pushIntegritySection(out, err, p.modifiedBlocks, o.idMatcherFactory, cwd);
   731	  if (!o.write) {
   732	    out.push(`\nRESULT: MERGED (dry-run; ${jobs.length} module(s)) — pass --write to apply`);
   733	    return { code: 0, out, err };
   734	  }
   735	
   736	  // --- phase 2: stage (this run's temps only — preflight proved none pre-existed) ---
   737	  const staged = [];
   738	  try {
   739	    for (const j of jobs) {
   740	      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(j.store), { recursive: true });
   741	      ops.writeFileSync(j.tmp, j.text);
   742	      staged.push(j.tmp);
   743	    }
   744	  } catch (e) {
   745	    for (const t of staged) { try { ops.rmSync(t, { force: true }); } catch { /* best effort */ } }
   746	    err.push(`archive: staging failed (${e.message}) — stores untouched, this run's temp files removed`);
   747	    return { code: 1, out, err };
   748	  }
   749	
   750	  // --- phase 3: commit (per-file atomic renames, sorted order; no rollback on mid-commit failure) ---
   751	  const committed = [];
   752	  for (const j of jobs) {
   753	    try { ops.renameSync(j.tmp, j.store); committed.push(j); }
   754	    catch (e) {
   755	      err.push(`archive: COMMIT FAILED at ${j.suffix} (${e.message})`);
   756	      err.push(`  committed:     ${committed.map((c) => c.suffix).join(', ') || '(none)'}`);
   757	      const remaining = jobs.filter((x) => !committed.includes(x));
   758	      err.push(`  NOT committed: ${remaining.map((x) => x.suffix).join(', ')}`);
   759	      err.push(`  temp files remaining for manual completion: ${remaining.map((x) => x.tmp).join(', ')}`);
   760	      return { code: 1, out, err };
   761	    }
   762	  }
   763	
   764	  // --- phase 4: move (only with explicit --changes-dir) ---
   765	  let moved = null;
   766	  if (o.changesDirExplicit) {
   767	    try { moved = archiveChangeDir(changesDir, o.change, new Date(), ops); }
   768	    catch (e) { err.push(`archive: stores committed but the change-dir move failed: ${e.message} — rerun to complete`); return { code: 1, out, err }; }
   769	    if (moved === null) { err.push('archive: stores committed but the change dir vanished before the move'); return { code: 1, out, err }; }
   770	  }
   771	  out.push(`\nRESULT: MERGED — ${jobs.length} module store(s) rewritten${moved ? `; change archived → ${moved}` : ''}`);
   772	  return { code: 0, out, err };
   773	}
   774	
   775	// print the CAS stamp line for a store file's current content
   776	const STAMP_USAGE = 'usage: apriori stamp <store-file>';
   777	function stampCli(argv) {
   778	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'stamp', usage: STAMP_USAGE, positionals: 1, flags: {} }, (f, pos) => {
   779	    const p = pos[0];
   780	    if (!fs.existsSync(p)) { console.log('<!-- apriori-base: new -->'); return 0; }
   781	    if (fs.statSync(p).isDirectory()) { console.error(`stamp: ${p} is a directory, not a store file`); return 2; }
   782	    let text;
   783	    try { text = fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'); }
   784	    catch (e) { console.error(`stamp: cannot read ${p}: ${e.message}`); return 2; }
   785	    console.log(`<!-- apriori-base: ${fingerprint(text)} -->`);
   786	    return 0;
   787	  });
   788	}
   789	
   790	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori archive --store <f> --delta <f> --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>] [--no-cas]\n   or: apriori archive --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>] [--no-cas]';
   791	
   792	function cli(argv, deps = {}) {
   793	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'archive', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   794	    flags: { '--store': 'value', '--delta': 'value', '--change': 'value', '--write': 'flag', '--changes-dir': 'value', '--no-cas': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
   795	    const a = { store: f['--store'] || null, delta: f['--delta'] || null, change: f['--change'] || null,
   796	      write: !!f['--write'], changesDir: f['--changes-dir'] || null, noCas: !!f['--no-cas'] };
   797	  // high-level form: --change alone (no --store/--delta) drives the whole change
   798	    if (a.change && !a.store && !a.delta) {
   799	      const r = archiveChange({ cwd: process.cwd(), change: a.change, write: a.write,
   800	        changesDir: a.changesDir || undefined, changesDirExplicit: !!a.changesDir, noCas: a.noCas,
   801	        idMatcherFactory: deps.idMatcherFactory });
   802	      for (const l of r.out) console.log(l);
   803	      for (const l of r.err) console.error(l);
   804	      return r.code;
   805	    }
   806	    if (a.change && (a.store || a.delta) && !(a.store && a.delta)) {
   807	      console.error('usage: apriori archive --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]  (high-level form)\n   or: apriori archive --store <f> --delta <f> --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]  (single-file form)\n(--change cannot be combined with only one of --store/--delta)');
   808	      return 2;
   809	    }
   810	    if (!a.store || !a.delta || !a.change) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   811	    const vEarly = require('./resolve').validateChangeName(a.change);
   812	    if (!vEarly.ok) { console.error(`archive: invalid change name '${a.change}' (${vEarly.kind}) — bare kebab-case, not date-prefixed, not a reserved name`); return 2; }
   813	    if (a.changesDir && !fs.existsSync(path.join(a.changesDir, a.change))) {
   814	      console.error(`archive: change '${a.change}' not found under ${a.changesDir} — nothing to move`); return 2;
   815	    }
   816	    // first archive in a repo: the store file may not exist yet — start from an empty store
   817	    const storeExists = fs.existsSync(a.store);
   818	    const storeText = storeExists ? fs.readFileSync(a.store, 'utf8') : '';
   819	    if (!storeExists) console.log(`store ${a.store} does not exist yet — will be created${a.write ? '' : ' on --write'}`);
   820	    const deltaText = fs.readFileSync(a.delta, 'utf8');
   821	    const { delta, stamp, problems } = parseDeltaStrict(deltaText);
   822	    if (problems.length) {
   823	      for (const p of problems) console.error(`archive: ${a.delta}: ${p}`);
   824	      console.log('\nRESULT: MALFORMED DELTA — nothing written');
   825	      return 1;
   826	    }
   827	    // Guard against a silent no-op: a delta with real content that parsed to zero operations
   828	    // is almost always a format mismatch (wrong heading level/keyword), not an empty change.
   829	    if (deltaText.trim() && deltaOpCount(delta) === 0) {
   830	      console.error(`archive: parsed 0 delta operations from ${a.delta} — it has content but no recognized operations.`);
   831	      console.error('  expected `## ADDED|MODIFIED|REMOVED Requirements` (h2) with `### Requirement: <name>` (h3) blocks,');
   832	      console.error('  or `## RENAMED Requirements` with `- Old -> New` lines. Nothing written.');
   833	      return 1;
   834	    }
   835	    // AM-32 (4.0): mutation ops without a stamp are denied unless visibly waived
   836	    if (!stamp && mutationOpCount(delta) > 0) {
   837	      const w = casWaiver(process.cwd(), a.noCas);
   838	      if (w.problem || !w.waiver) {
   839	        console.error(`archive: ${w.problem ? w.problem + ' — a broken config never equals a waiver (denied)' : unstampedWarning(a.delta) + ' — denied by default; waive with --no-cas or a | cas | optional | config row'}`);
   840	        console.log('\nRESULT: FAILED PREFLIGHT — nothing written');
   841	        return 1;
   842	      }
   843	      console.log(`warning: ${unstampedWarning(a.delta)}`);
   844	      console.log(`note: cas waived by ${w.waiver}`);
   845	    }
   846	    // CAS: a present base stamp must match the store the delta is about to merge into (AM-24/AM-26)
   847	    if (stamp) {
   848	      const actual = storeExists ? fingerprint(storeText) : 'new';
   849	      if (stamp !== actual) {
   850	        console.error(`archive: base mismatch for ${a.store}: delta expects ${stamp}, store is ${actual} — nothing written (serialize per §4.11)`);
   851	        return 1;
   852	      }
   853	    }
   854	    const { store, merged, modified, deprecated, renamed, conflicts, unchanged } = merge(storeText, delta, a.change);
   855	
   856	    for (const [label, ids] of [['merged (ADDED)', merged], ['modified (MODIFIED)', modified], ['deprecated (REMOVED)', deprecated], ['renamed (RENAMED)', renamed], ['already merged (no-op)', unchanged]])
   857	      if (ids.length) console.log(`${label}: ${ids.join(', ')}`);
   858	
   859	    if (conflicts.length) {
   860	      console.log('\nCONFLICTS (stop — open a ledger issue, human resolves):');
   861	      for (const c of conflicts) console.log(`  ✗ ${c}`);
   862	      console.log('\nRESULT: CONFLICT — nothing written');
   863	      return 1;
   864	    }
   865	    if (a.write) {
   866	      // Transactional ordering: stage the store to a temp file, do the (atomic) dir move, then
   867	      // commit the store via rename. If the move fails, the temp is discarded and the store on
   868	      // disk stays byte-for-byte untouched — never a half-completed archive.
   869	      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(a.store), { recursive: true });
   870	      const tmp = a.store + '.tmp-archive';
   871	      fs.writeFileSync(tmp, renderStore(storeText, store));
   872	      let moved = null;
   873	      if (a.changesDir) {
   874	        try { moved = archiveChangeDir(a.changesDir, a.change, new Date()); }
   875	        catch (e) { fs.rmSync(tmp, { force: true }); console.error(`archive: ${e.message} — store left untouched`); return 1; }
   876	        if (moved === null) { fs.rmSync(tmp, { force: true }); console.error(`archive: change dir vanished mid-run — store left untouched`); return 1; }
   877	      }
   878	      fs.renameSync(tmp, a.store);
   879	      console.log(`\nRESULT: MERGED — ${a.store} rewritten${moved ? `; change archived → ${moved}` : ''}`);
   880	    } else {
   881	      console.log(`\nRESULT: MERGED (dry-run; ${store.size} requirements in result) — pass --write to apply`);
   882	    }
   883	    return 0;
   884	  });
   885	}
   886	module.exports = { parseRequirements, parseRequirementsStrict, parseDelta, parseDeltaStrict, deltaOpCount, mutationOpCount, unstampedWarning,
   887	  compareModifiedBlock, formatIntegrityHuman,
   888	  merge, renderStore, archiveStamp, archiveChangeDir, archiveChange, CHANGE_NAME_RE,
   889	  fingerprint, parseStamp, containsReal, discoverDeltas, buildProjection,
   890	  DEPRECATED_RE, stripDeprecatedBlocks, stampCli, cli };
     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * apriori gate — aggregate the MECHANICAL exit conditions for one change into one exit code.
     4	 * 0 = PASS · 1 = BLOCKED · 2 = the evaluation itself is untrustworthy.
     5	 * Strictly read-only. PASS covers machine checks only — human gates remain human.
     6	 */
     7	const fs = require('fs');
     8	const path = require('path');
     9	const { spawnSync } = require('child_process');
    10	const { parseFlowState, parseLedger } = require('./status');
    11	const sr = require('./spec-runner');
    12	const { verify, configTestCmd } = sr;
    13	const { CHANGE_NAME_RE, containsReal } = require('./archive-merge');
    14	const { resolveChange, configCas, configCasProblem } = require('./resolve');
    15	const { resolveIdPattern } = require('./config');
    16	const { withStrict } = require('./args');
    17	
    18	const STEP_ENUM = ['STEP0', 'STEP1', 'STEP2', 'STEP3', 'STEP4', 'STEP5', 'STEP6',
    19	  'INTENT-CARD', 'SPIKE', 'EXTRACTION', 'DONE', 'ABANDONED'];
    20	const TIER_ENUM = ['trivial', 'medium', 'large'];
    21	const CAVEAT = 'mechanical checks only; human gates remain human';
    22	
    23	function err(res, msg) { res.errors.push(msg); res.result = 'ERROR'; res.code = 2; return res; }
    24	
    25	
    26	// --- individual checks; each returns {id, status: 'pass'|'blocked'|'n/a', detail} ---
    27	
    28	function checkFlowState(state, name) {
    29	  for (const key of ['change', 'tier', 'track', 'lineage', 'current-step']) {
    30	    const v = state[key];
    31	    if (v === undefined || v === '') return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: required key '${key}' missing` };
    32	    if (v.includes('<') || v.includes('>')) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: '${key}' is an unfilled placeholder (${v})` };
    33	  }
    34	  if (state.change !== name) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'change' is '${state.change}', expected '${name}'` };
    35	  if (!STEP_ENUM.includes(state['current-step'])) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'current-step' '${state['current-step']}' not in the legal vocabulary` };
    36	  if (!TIER_ENUM.includes(state.tier)) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'tier' '${state.tier}' not in {trivial, medium, large}` };
    37	  return { id: 'C3', status: 'pass', detail: `legal (tier ${state.tier}, ${state['current-step']})` };
    38	}
    39	
    40	function checkTasks(dir, tier) {
    41	  const p = path.join(dir, 'tasks.md');
    42	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    43	    return tier === 'trivial'
    44	      ? { id: 'C2', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no tasks.md — trivial tier has no STEP2' }
    45	      : { id: 'C2', status: 'blocked', detail: `tasks.md missing at ${p}` };
    46	  }
    47	  const open = (fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8').match(/^\s*-\s\[\s\]/gm) || []).length;
    48	  return open
    49	    ? { id: 'C2', status: 'blocked', detail: `tasks.md has ${open} unchecked box(es)` }
    50	    : { id: 'C2', status: 'pass', detail: 'all tasks checked' };
    51	}
    52	
    53	// the ledger vocabulary, one classifier shared by C4 and the corpus test (GT-15).
    54	// leading token, case-insensitive; rejected-verified BEFORE rejected (alternation order).
    55	const STATUS_RE = /^(open|fixed|verified|rejected-verified|rejected|advisory-acked|waived)\b/i;
    56	function classifyStatus(status) {
    57	  const m = STATUS_RE.exec(status);
    58	  if (!m) return { legal: false, terminal: false, needsReason: false, hasReason: false, isWaived: false };
    59	  const tok = m[1].toLowerCase();
    60	  const needsReason = tok === 'rejected' || tok === 'rejected-verified' || tok === 'waived';
    61	  return {
    62	    legal: true,
    63	    terminal: tok === 'verified' || tok === 'rejected-verified' || tok === 'waived' || tok === 'advisory-acked',
    64	    needsReason,
    65	    hasReason: !needsReason || /\w/.test(status.slice(m[0].length)),
    66	    isWaived: tok === 'waived',
    67	    token: tok,
    68	  };
    69	}
    70	
    71	const escapeRe = (s) => s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
    72	
    73	// gates: entries from a flow-state text — the block runs from the unindented `gates:` line
    74	// to the next unindented top-level key; entries start `- `, continuation lines attach.
    75	function gatesEntries(flowText) {
    76	  const start = flowText.search(/^gates:/m);
    77	  if (start < 0) return [];
    78	  const rest = flowText.slice(start + 'gates:'.length);
    79	  const end = rest.search(/^[A-Za-z][\w-]*:/m);
    80	  const block = end < 0 ? rest : rest.slice(0, end);
    81	  const entries = [];
    82	  for (const line of block.split('\n')) {
    83	    if (/^\s*-\s/.test(line)) entries.push(line.trim());
    84	    else if (entries.length && line.trim()) entries[entries.length - 1] += ' ' + line.trim();
    85	  }
    86	  return entries;
    87	}
    88	
    89	// a waive is a HUMAN act: one single gates: entry must carry the row ID as an exact
    90	// token (LS-1 never matches inside LS-10) AND the word waive/waived.
    91	function waiveEvidence(flowText, id) {
    92	  const idRe = new RegExp(`(^|[^A-Za-z0-9-])${escapeRe(id)}([^A-Za-z0-9-]|$)`);
    93	  return gatesEntries(flowText).some((e) => idRe.test(e) && /waiv/i.test(e));
    94	}
    95	
    96	function checkLedger(tier, stage, dir) {
    97	  const p = path.join(dir, 'review', 'issues.md');
    98	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    99	    return tier === 'trivial'
   100	      ? { id: 'C4', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no ledger — trivial tier may never open one' }
   101	      : { id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: `ledger missing at ${p}` };
   102	  }
   103	  const rows = parseLedger(fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'));
   104	  const fsPath = dir ? path.join(dir, 'flow-state.md') : null;
   105	  const flowText = fsPath && fs.existsSync(fsPath) ? fs.readFileSync(fsPath, 'utf8') : '';
   106	  const bad = [];
   107	  for (const r of rows) {
   108	    const c = classifyStatus(r.status);
   109	    if (!c.legal) { bad.push(`${r.id}: '${r.status}' is not in the ledger vocabulary`); continue; }
   110	    if (c.needsReason && !c.hasReason) { bad.push(`${r.id} is ${c.token} without a reason`); continue; }
   111	    if (c.isWaived && !waiveEvidence(flowText, r.id)) {
   112	      bad.push(`${r.id} is waived without a gates: entry recording the human decision`); continue;
   113	    }
   114	    if (c.token === 'open') { bad.push(`${r.id} is open`); continue; }
   115	    if (stage === 'archived' && !c.terminal) {
   116	      bad.push(c.token === 'fixed'
   117	        ? `${r.id} is fixed but never verified — reviewer must verify, or the human waives it`
   118	        : `${r.id} is rejected without reviewer concurrence — flip to rejected-verified, or the human waives it`);
   119	    }
   120	  }
   121	  return bad.length
   122	    ? { id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: bad.join('; ') }
   123	    : { id: 'C4', status: 'pass', detail: `${rows.length} row(s), none blocking` };
   124	}
   125	
   126	// the bundle review/ dir is the shared evidence root for C4 and C5 — when present it must be
   127	// a REAL contained directory (symlinked/escaping/non-dir entries block, never read through)
   128	function reviewDirDefect(dir) {
   129	  const rd = path.join(dir, 'review');
   130	  let st;
   131	  try { st = fs.lstatSync(rd); } catch { return null; }  // lstat, not exists: a dangling symlink is a defect, not absence
   132	  if (st.isSymbolicLink()) return `review is a symlink: ${rd}`;
   133	  if (!st.isDirectory()) return `review is not a directory: ${rd}`;
   134	  if (!containsReal(dir, rd)) return `review escapes the change dir: ${rd}`;
   135	  return null;
   136	}
   137	
   138	function checkEvidence(dir) {
   139	  const rd = path.join(dir, 'review');
   140	  const docs = [];
   141	  if (fs.existsSync(rd)) {
   142	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(rd, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   143	      if (!e.name.endsWith('.md') || e.name === 'issues.md' || /-raw(\.|$)/.test(e.name.replace(/\.md$/, ''))) continue;
   144	      if (e.name.includes('-raw')) continue;
   145	      const p = path.join(rd, e.name);
   146	      if (fs.lstatSync(p).isSymbolicLink())
   147	        return { id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: `evidence doc is a symlink: ${e.name}` };
   148	      if (!fs.lstatSync(p).isFile()) continue;
   149	      docs.push(p);
   150	    }
   151	  }
   152	  const missing = [];
   153	  let verdictDocs = 0;
   154	  for (const doc of docs) {
   155	    if (!/^VERDICT:/m.test(fs.readFileSync(doc, 'utf8'))) continue;
   156	    verdictDocs++;
   157	    const stem = path.basename(doc).replace(/\.md$/, '');
   158	    const hasRaw = fs.existsSync(rd) && fs.readdirSync(rd).some((n) => {
   159	      if (!n.startsWith(stem + '-raw')) return false;
   160	      const rp = path.join(rd, n);
   161	      return fs.lstatSync(rp).isFile();
   162	    });
   163	    if (!hasRaw) missing.push(path.basename(doc));
   164	  }
   165	  if (missing.length) return { id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: `verdict doc(s) without a raw archive: ${missing.join(', ')}` };
   166	  return { id: 'C5', status: 'pass', detail: `${verdictDocs} review doc(s), every verdict has raw evidence` };
   167	}
   168	
   169	function checkKb(cwd, dir) {
   170	  const specsDir = path.join(dir, 'specs');
   171	  const modules = new Set();
   172	  const walk = (d, base) => {
   173	    if (!fs.existsSync(d)) return;
   174	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   175	      const p = path.join(d, e.name);
   176	      if (e.isDirectory()) walk(p, base);
   177	      else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) modules.add(path.relative(base, p).split(path.sep)[0]);
   178	    }
   179	  };
   180	  walk(specsDir, specsDir);
   180	  walk(specsDir, specsDir);
   181	  if (!modules.size) return { id: 'C6', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no delta specs to map' };
   182	
   183	  // build the module → truth index (c6-truth-binding): a truth doc binds by its
   184	  // declared store-module (default = basename), never by a filename assumption
   185	  const truthDir = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'truth');
   186	  const index = new Map();                 // module → { text, sourceFiles, basename }
   187	  const conflicts = new Map();             // module → "declared by A and B"
   188	  if (fs.existsSync(truthDir)) {
   189	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(truthDir).sort()) {
   190	      if (!e.endsWith('.md')) continue;
   191	      const text = fs.readFileSync(path.join(truthDir, e), 'utf8');
   192	      const h2 = text.search(/^##\s/m);
   193	      const header = h2 >= 0 ? text.slice(0, h2) : text;
   194	      const smM = header.match(/^store-module:\s*(.+)$/m);
   195	      const sfM = header.match(/^source-files:\s*(.+)$/m);
   196	      const mods = smM ? smM[1].trim().split(/\s+/) : [e.replace(/\.md$/, '')];
   197	      const sourceFiles = sfM ? sfM[1].trim().split(/\s+/) : null;   // null = default lib/<module>.js
   198	      for (const m of mods) {
   199	        if (index.has(m)) conflicts.set(m, `${m}: declared by both ${index.get(m).basename} and ${e}`);
   200	        else index.set(m, { text, sourceFiles, basename: e });
   201	      }
   202	    }
   203	  }
   204	  const stripFencesKb = (t) => t.replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, '');
   205	  const blocked = [], notes = [];
   206	  let checked = 0;
   207	  for (const m of [...modules].sort()) {
   208	    if (conflicts.has(m)) { blocked.push(conflicts.get(m)); continue; }
   209	    const entry = index.get(m);
   210	    if (!entry) { notes.push(`${m}: no truth doc`); continue; }
   211	    const noFence = stripFencesKb(entry.text);
   212	    const sc = noFence.match(/^source-commit:\s+(\S+)/m);
   213	    if (!sc) {
   214	      const attempt = noFence.split('\n').some((l) => l.includes('source-commit:') && !/^source-commit:\s+\S+/.test(l));
   215	      notes.push(attempt
   216	        ? `${m}: source-commit is not in the canonical fence-outside line-start form 'source-commit: <ref>'`
   217	        : `${m}: truth doc has no source-commit`);
   218	      continue;
   219	    }
   220	    const ref = sc[1];
   221	    const explicit = entry.sourceFiles !== null;
   222	    const declared = explicit ? entry.sourceFiles : [`lib/${m}.js`];
   223	    // an explicit declaration is a complete promise: every token must be verifiable
   224	    const usable = [];
   225	    let tokenBad = null;
   226	    for (const rel of declared) {
   227	      // an explicit token is a repo-relative promise: reject absolute/backslash/empty/. or .. segments
   228	      if (explicit && (rel === '' || path.isAbsolute(rel) || rel.includes('\\') ||
   229	          rel.split('/').some((seg) => seg === '' || seg === '.' || seg === '..'))) {
   230	        tokenBad = `${m}: malformed source-files token (must be a normalized repo-relative path): ${rel}`; break;
   231	      }
   232	      const abs = path.join(cwd, rel);
   233	      let st = null; try { st = fs.lstatSync(abs); } catch { /* absent */ }
   234	      if (!st) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token missing: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   235	      if (st.isSymbolicLink()) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token is a symlink: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   236	      if (!containsReal(cwd, abs)) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token escapes the repo: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   237	      if (!st.isFile() && !st.isDirectory()) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token is neither file nor directory: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   238	      usable.push(rel);
   239	    }
   240	    if (tokenBad) { blocked.push(tokenBad); continue; }
   241	    if (!usable.length) { notes.push(`${m}: no ${declared.join(' / ')} to compare`); continue; }
   242	    const g = spawnSync('git', ['-C', cwd, 'log', '--oneline', `${ref}..HEAD`, '--', ...usable], { encoding: 'utf8' });
   243	    if (g.error) { notes.push(`${m}: git unavailable (${g.error.message})`); continue; }
   244	    if (g.status !== 0) { notes.push(`${m}: git failed (${(g.stderr || '').split('\n')[0]})`); continue; }
   245	    const n = g.stdout.trim() ? g.stdout.trim().split('\n').length : 0;
   246	    if (n > 0) blocked.push(`${m}: ${n} commit(s) since ${ref}`);
   247	    else checked++;
   248	  }
   249	  if (blocked.length) return { id: 'C6', status: 'blocked', detail: blocked.join('; ') };
   250	  if (checked > 0) return { id: 'C6', status: 'pass', detail: `${checked} module stamp(s) up to date${notes.length ? `; ${notes.join('; ')}` : ''}` };
   251	  return { id: 'C6', status: 'n/a', detail: notes.join('; ') };
   252	}
   253	
   254	function checkBinding(cwd, name, stage, testCmd, idPattern) {
   255	  const run = stage === 'in-flight'
   256	    ? verify({ change: name, cwd, testCmd, idPattern })
   257	    : verify({ specs: [path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs')], cwd, testCmd, idPattern });
   258	  if (run.errors.length) return { infra: run.errors };
   259	  const v = run.verdict;
   260	  const clean = v.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0;
   261	  // in-flight runs carry the informative store summary (change-scoped verdict, GT-26/27)
   262	  const sr2 = run.storeReport;
   263	  const suffix = sr2
   264	    ? `; store: ${sr2.boundRed.length} red, ${sr2.unbound.length} unbound, ${sr2.orphan.length} orphan, ${sr2.unidentified.length} unidentified, ${sr2.unattributedFailures.count} unattributed, ${sr2.duplicates.length} duplicate(s) outstanding`
   265	    : '';
   266	  const label0 = sr2 ? `${stage}, change-scoped` : stage;
   267	  if (clean) return { check: { id: 'C1', status: 'pass', detail: `verify GREEN (${label0})${suffix}` }, projection: run.projection };
   268	  const parts = [];
   269	  for (const [label, arr] of [['red', v.boundRed], ['unbound', v.unbound], ['orphan', v.orphan], ['unidentified', v.unidentified], ['unattributed-failures', v.unattributed || []], ['duplicate-IDs', run.duplicates]])
   270	    if (arr.length) parts.push(`${arr.length} ${label}`);
   271	  return { check: { id: 'C1', status: 'blocked', detail: `verify GAPS: ${parts.join(', ')}${suffix}` }, projection: run.projection };
   272	}
   273	
   274	
   275	// The same shape as checkBinding, for the path where no test command exists. C1 cannot run,
   276	// but everything C1 was ALSO producing for its neighbours still can: C7 needs the projection,
   277	// and a broken id-pattern is still a broken config. Nothing here spawns a process.
   278	function checkBindingSkipped(cwd, name, stage, idPattern) {
   279	  // a broken pattern is a broken CONFIG, not an absent one — it stays an evaluation error
   280	  // (GT-24 keeps holding). Compile-checking is in-process; no scenario is ever MATCHED here,
   281	  // so no matcher child is spawned and GT-25's runtime precondition cannot arise.
   282	  const idp = resolveIdPattern(cwd, idPattern);
   283	  if (idp.error) return { infra: [idp.error] };
   284	  const detail = 'skipped — no test command (pass --test-cmd or add a test-cmd row to '
   285	    + 'apriori/process-config.md); the binding check did not run';
   286	  const c1 = { id: 'C1', status: 'skipped', detail };
   287	  // archived: the deltas are already merged and C7 is n/a — building a projection could only
   288	  // manufacture a false block, so it is never built at all.
   289	  if (stage === 'archived') return { check: c1, projection: null };
   290	  const b = sr.currentProjectionBuilder()(name, cwd);
   291	  if (b.errors && b.errors.length) return { infra: b.errors };
   292	  // fail closed: no trustworthy texts is untrustworthy even when nothing said why. Reporting
   293	  // it with an empty errors list would be an ERROR that cannot explain itself.
   294	  if (!b.texts) return { infra: ['projection produced no trustworthy texts and reported no error — refusing to judge'] };
   295	  return { check: c1, projection: b.projection };
   296	}
   297	
   298	// The test command has three fates, and "broken" is not "absent". A flag that is present but
   299	// empty (or all whitespace) is an operator error in THIS invocation and must never fall back
   300	// to the config; an empty config VALUE, by contrast, was already normalised to "no such row"
   301	// by the shared reader (config.js), so gate sees an absence and treats it as one.
   302	function resolveTestCmd(opts, cwd) {
   303	  const flag = opts.testCmd;
   304	  if (flag !== undefined && flag !== null) {
   305	    if (typeof flag !== 'string') return { kind: 'error', error: `--test-cmd must be a string (got ${Array.isArray(flag) ? 'array' : typeof flag})` };
   306	    if (flag.trim() === '') return { kind: 'error', error: 'empty --test-cmd — pass a command or omit the flag' };
   307	    return { kind: 'ok', value: flag };
   308	  }
   309	  const c = configTestCmd(cwd);
   310	  if (c && c.error) return { kind: 'error', error: `verify: ${c.error}` };
   311	  if (c) return { kind: 'ok', value: c };
   312	  return { kind: 'absent' };
   313	}
   314	
   315	// C7: unstamped mutation deltas are denied by default — a waiver is always visible (GT-16).
   316	// Archived stage: the deltas are already merged; there is nothing left to stamp-check.
   317	function checkCas(projection, stage, noCas, cwd) {
   318	  if (stage === 'archived') return { id: 'C7', status: 'n/a', detail: 'deltas already merged' };
   319	  const um = (projection && projection.unstampedMutations) || [];
   320	  if (!um.length) return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'every mutation delta is stamped' };  // nothing to consult — silent pass (GT-16)
   321	  if (noCas) return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'waived (--no-cas)' };
   322	  const problem = configCasProblem(cwd);                       // consumption-time only
   323	  if (problem) return { id: 'C7', status: 'blocked', detail: `${problem} — a broken config never equals a waiver` };
   324	  if (configCas(cwd) === 'optional') return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'waived (process-config cas: optional)' };
   325	  return { id: 'C7', status: 'blocked',
   326	    detail: `unstamped mutation delta(s): ${um.join(', ')} — run: apriori stamp <store-file> (or waive with --no-cas / a process-config cas: optional row)` };
   327	}
   328	
   329	// The whole evaluation. → { code, stage, checks, result, blocked, errors }
   330	function runGate(opts) {
   331	  const cwd = opts.cwd || process.cwd();
   332	  const res = { code: 0, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'PASS', blocked: 0, errors: [], change: opts.change || null };
   333	  if (!opts.change) return err(res, USAGE);   // the ONE usage constant — every path lists the same flags
   334	  const nameCheck = require('./resolve').validateChangeName(opts.change);
   335	  if (!nameCheck.ok) return err(res, `invalid change name '${opts.change}' (${nameCheck.kind}) — bare kebab-case, not date-prefixed, not a reserved name`);
   336	  const loc = resolveChange(cwd, opts.change);
   337	  if (loc.error) return err(res, loc.error);
   338	  res.stage = loc.stage;
   339	  const flowPath = path.join(loc.dir, 'flow-state.md');
   340	  // mapping m1: a hotfix bundle is not this gate's object. It is refused with a pointer at
   341	  // its own preflight rather than adapted — the seven checks below stay exactly as they are.
   342	  const hotfixPath = path.join(loc.dir, 'hotfix-state.md');
   343	  if (fs.existsSync(hotfixPath)) {
   344	    return err(res, fs.existsSync(flowPath)
   345	      ? `'${opts.change}' carries BOTH flow-state.md and hotfix-state.md — a bundle has one identity; delete the one that does not belong`
   346	      : `'${opts.change}' is a hotfix bundle, not a formal change — its mechanical exit conditions are \`apriori hotfix archive ${opts.change}\` (the gate's seven checks do not apply to the lane)`);
   347	  }
   348	  if (!fs.existsSync(flowPath)) return err(res, `no readable flow-state.md at ${flowPath} — tier-aware checks are impossible`);
   349	  let state;
   350	  try { state = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(flowPath, 'utf8')); }
   351	  catch (e) { return err(res, `flow-state unreadable: ${e.message}`); }
   352	  const tc = resolveTestCmd(opts, cwd);
   353	  if (tc.kind === 'error') return err(res, tc.error);
   354	
   355	  const idPattern = opts.idPattern === undefined ? null : opts.idPattern;
   356	  const b = tc.kind === 'absent'
   357	    ? checkBindingSkipped(cwd, opts.change, loc.stage, idPattern)
   358	    : checkBinding(cwd, opts.change, loc.stage, tc.value, idPattern);
   359	  if (b.infra) { for (const e of b.infra) res.errors.push(`verify: ${e}`); res.result = 'ERROR'; res.code = 2; return res; }
   360	  res.checks.push(b.check);
   361	  const tier = TIER_ENUM.includes(state.tier) ? state.tier : null;
   362	  res.checks.push(checkTasks(loc.dir, tier));
   363	  res.checks.push(checkFlowState(state, opts.change));
   364	  const rdDefect = reviewDirDefect(loc.dir);
   365	  if (rdDefect) {
   366	    res.checks.push({ id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
   367	    res.checks.push({ id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
   368	  } else {
   369	    res.checks.push(checkLedger(tier, loc.stage, loc.dir));
   370	    res.checks.push(checkEvidence(loc.dir));
   371	  }
   372	
   373	  res.checks.push(checkKb(cwd, loc.dir));
   374	  res.checks.push(checkCas(b.projection, loc.stage, !!opts.noCas, cwd));
   375	
   376	  // ERROR(2) > BLOCKED(1) > INCOMPLETE(3) > PASS(0). The ERROR rung is served by the early
   377	  // returns above, so only the lower three are decided here. `blocked` counts blocked only:
   378	  // an unrun check is not a block, and must not inflate the number a human reads.
   379	  res.blocked = res.checks.filter((c) => c.status === 'blocked').length;
   380	  const skipped = res.checks.some((c) => c.status === 'skipped');
   381	  res.result = res.blocked ? 'BLOCKED' : skipped ? 'INCOMPLETE' : 'PASS';
   382	  res.code = res.blocked ? 1 : skipped ? 3 : 0;
   383	  return res;
   384	}
   385	
   386	function toJson(res) {
   387	  return { change: res.change, stage: res.stage, checks: res.checks, result: res.result, blocked: res.blocked, errors: res.errors };
   388	}
   389	
   390	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori gate --change <name> [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json] [--no-cas]';
   391	
   392	function cli(argv) {
   393	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'gate', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   394	    flags: { '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--id-pattern': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag', '--no-cas': 'flag' },
   395	    jsonError: (m) => JSON.stringify({ change: null, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'ERROR', blocked: 0, errors: [m] }, null, 2) }, (f) => {
   396	    const a = { change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: ('--test-cmd' in f) ? f['--test-cmd'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness — an empty flag is an error, never a config fallback
   397	      idPattern: ('--id-pattern' in f) ? f['--id-pattern'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness (GT-24 empty flag)
   398	      cwd: f['--cwd'] || process.cwd(), json: !!f['--json'], noCas: !!f['--no-cas'] };
   399	    const res = runGate(a);
   400	    if (a.json) { console.log(JSON.stringify(toJson(res), null, 2)); return res.code; }
   401	    for (const e of res.errors) console.error('gate: ' + e);
   402	    const mark = { pass: '✓', blocked: '✗', 'n/a': '–', skipped: '○' };
   403	    for (const c of res.checks) console.log(`${mark[c.status]} ${c.id} ${c.status === 'blocked' ? 'BLOCKED — ' : ''}${c.detail}`);
   404	    if (res.code === 0) console.log(`\nGATE: PASS — ${CAVEAT}`);
   405	    else if (res.code === 1) console.log(`\nGATE: BLOCKED (${res.blocked} item(s))`);
   406	    else if (res.code === 3) console.log('\nGATE: INCOMPLETE — C1 did not run; PASS was not reached');
   407	    return res.code;
   408	  });
   409	}

   790	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori archive --store <f> --delta <f> --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>] [--no-cas]\n   or: apriori archive --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>] [--no-cas]';
   791	
   792	function cli(argv, deps = {}) {
   793	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'archive', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   794	    flags: { '--store': 'value', '--delta': 'value', '--change': 'value', '--write': 'flag', '--changes-dir': 'value', '--no-cas': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
   795	    const a = { store: f['--store'] || null, delta: f['--delta'] || null, change: f['--change'] || null,
   796	      write: !!f['--write'], changesDir: f['--changes-dir'] || null, noCas: !!f['--no-cas'] };
   797	  // high-level form: --change alone (no --store/--delta) drives the whole change
   798	    if (a.change && !a.store && !a.delta) {
   799	      const r = archiveChange({ cwd: process.cwd(), change: a.change, write: a.write,
   800	        changesDir: a.changesDir || undefined, changesDirExplicit: !!a.changesDir, noCas: a.noCas,
   801	        idMatcherFactory: deps.idMatcherFactory });

   803	      for (const l of r.err) console.error(l);
   804	      return r.code;
   805	    }
   806	    if (a.change && (a.store || a.delta) && !(a.store && a.delta)) {
   807	      console.error('usage: apriori archive --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]  (high-level form)\n   or: apriori archive --store <f> --delta <f> --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]  (single-file form)\n(--change cannot be combined with only one of --store/--delta)');
   808	      return 2;
   809	    }
   810	    if (!a.store || !a.delta || !a.change) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   811	    const vEarly = require('./resolve').validateChangeName(a.change);
   812	    if (!vEarly.ok) { console.error(`archive: invalid change name '${a.change}' (${vEarly.kind}) — bare kebab-case, not date-prefixed, not a reserved name`); return 2; }
   813	    if (a.changesDir && !fs.existsSync(path.join(a.changesDir, a.change))) {
   814	      console.error(`archive: change '${a.change}' not found under ${a.changesDir} — nothing to move`); return 2;
   815	    }
   816	    // first archive in a repo: the store file may not exist yet — start from an empty store
   817	    const storeExists = fs.existsSync(a.store);
   818	    const storeText = storeExists ? fs.readFileSync(a.store, 'utf8') : '';
   819	    if (!storeExists) console.log(`store ${a.store} does not exist yet — will be created${a.write ? '' : ' on --write'}`);

   821	    const { delta, stamp, problems } = parseDeltaStrict(deltaText);
   822	    if (problems.length) {
   823	      for (const p of problems) console.error(`archive: ${a.delta}: ${p}`);
   824	      console.log('\nRESULT: MALFORMED DELTA — nothing written');
   825	      return 1;
   826	    }
   827	    // Guard against a silent no-op: a delta with real content that parsed to zero operations
   828	    // is almost always a format mismatch (wrong heading level/keyword), not an empty change.
   829	    if (deltaText.trim() && deltaOpCount(delta) === 0) {
   830	      console.error(`archive: parsed 0 delta operations from ${a.delta} — it has content but no recognized operations.`);

   833	      return 1;

   838	      if (w.problem || !w.waiver) {

   855	
   856	    for (const [label, ids] of [['merged (ADDED)', merged], ['modified (MODIFIED)', modified], ['deprecated (REMOVED)', deprecated], ['renamed (RENAMED)', renamed], ['already merged (no-op)', unchanged]])

   866	      // Transactional ordering: stage the store to a temp file, do the (atomic) dir move, then
   867	      // commit the store via rename. If the move fails, the temp is discarded and the store on
   868	      // disk stays byte-for-byte untouched — never a half-completed archive.

   874	        try { moved = archiveChangeDir(a.changesDir, a.change, new Date()); }

   879	      console.log(`\nRESULT: MERGED — ${a.store} rewritten${moved ? `; change archived → ${moved}` : ''}`);
   880	    } else {
   881	      console.log(`\nRESULT: MERGED (dry-run; ${store.size} requirements in result) — pass --write to apply`);
   882	    }
   883	    return 0;
   884	  });

    18	const STEP_ENUM = ['STEP0', 'STEP1', 'STEP2', 'STEP3', 'STEP4', 'STEP5', 'STEP6',
    19	  'INTENT-CARD', 'SPIKE', 'EXTRACTION', 'DONE', 'ABANDONED'];

    21	const CAVEAT = 'mechanical checks only; human gates remain human';

    26	// --- individual checks; each returns {id, status: 'pass'|'blocked'|'n/a', detail} ---
    27	
    28	function checkFlowState(state, name) {
    29	  for (const key of ['change', 'tier', 'track', 'lineage', 'current-step']) {
    30	    const v = state[key];
    31	    if (v === undefined || v === '') return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: required key '${key}' missing` };
    32	    if (v.includes('<') || v.includes('>')) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: '${key}' is an unfilled placeholder (${v})` };
    33	  }
    34	  if (state.change !== name) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'change' is '${state.change}', expected '${name}'` };
    35	  if (!STEP_ENUM.includes(state['current-step'])) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'current-step' '${state['current-step']}' not in the legal vocabulary` };
    36	  if (!TIER_ENUM.includes(state.tier)) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'tier' '${state.tier}' not in {trivial, medium, large}` };
    37	  return { id: 'C3', status: 'pass', detail: `legal (tier ${state.tier}, ${state['current-step']})` };
    38	}

    40	function checkTasks(dir, tier) {
    41	  const p = path.join(dir, 'tasks.md');
    42	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    43	    return tier === 'trivial'
    44	      ? { id: 'C2', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no tasks.md — trivial tier has no STEP2' }
    45	      : { id: 'C2', status: 'blocked', detail: `tasks.md missing at ${p}` };
    46	  }

    48	  return open
    49	    ? { id: 'C2', status: 'blocked', detail: `tasks.md has ${open} unchecked box(es)` }
    50	    : { id: 'C2', status: 'pass', detail: 'all tasks checked' };
    51	}

    53	// the ledger vocabulary, one classifier shared by C4 and the corpus test (GT-15).
    54	// leading token, case-insensitive; rejected-verified BEFORE rejected (alternation order).
    55	const STATUS_RE = /^(open|fixed|verified|rejected-verified|rejected|advisory-acked|waived)\b/i;
    56	function classifyStatus(status) {
    57	  const m = STATUS_RE.exec(status);
    58	  if (!m) return { legal: false, terminal: false, needsReason: false, hasReason: false, isWaived: false };
    59	  const tok = m[1].toLowerCase();
    60	  const needsReason = tok === 'rejected' || tok === 'rejected-verified' || tok === 'waived';
    61	  return {
    62	    legal: true,
    63	    terminal: tok === 'verified' || tok === 'rejected-verified' || tok === 'waived' || tok === 'advisory-acked',
    64	    needsReason,
    65	    hasReason: !needsReason || /\w/.test(status.slice(m[0].length)),
    66	    isWaived: tok === 'waived',
    67	    token: tok,
    68	  };
    69	}
    70	
    71	const escapeRe = (s) => s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
    72	
    73	// gates: entries from a flow-state text — the block runs from the unindented `gates:` line
    74	// to the next unindented top-level key; entries start `- `, continuation lines attach.
    75	function gatesEntries(flowText) {
    76	  const start = flowText.search(/^gates:/m);
    77	  if (start < 0) return [];
    78	  const rest = flowText.slice(start + 'gates:'.length);
    79	  const end = rest.search(/^[A-Za-z][\w-]*:/m);
    80	  const block = end < 0 ? rest : rest.slice(0, end);
    81	  const entries = [];
    82	  for (const line of block.split('\n')) {
    83	    if (/^\s*-\s/.test(line)) entries.push(line.trim());
    84	    else if (entries.length && line.trim()) entries[entries.length - 1] += ' ' + line.trim();
    85	  }
    86	  return entries;
    87	}
    88	
    89	// a waive is a HUMAN act: one single gates: entry must carry the row ID as an exact
    90	// token (LS-1 never matches inside LS-10) AND the word waive/waived.
    91	function waiveEvidence(flowText, id) {
    92	  const idRe = new RegExp(`(^|[^A-Za-z0-9-])${escapeRe(id)}([^A-Za-z0-9-]|$)`);
    93	  return gatesEntries(flowText).some((e) => idRe.test(e) && /waiv/i.test(e));
    94	}

    96	function checkLedger(tier, stage, dir) {
    97	  const p = path.join(dir, 'review', 'issues.md');
    98	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    99	    return tier === 'trivial'
   100	      ? { id: 'C4', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no ledger — trivial tier may never open one' }
   101	      : { id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: `ledger missing at ${p}` };
   102	  }
   103	  const rows = parseLedger(fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'));
   104	  const fsPath = dir ? path.join(dir, 'flow-state.md') : null;
   105	  const flowText = fsPath && fs.existsSync(fsPath) ? fs.readFileSync(fsPath, 'utf8') : '';
   106	  const bad = [];
   107	  for (const r of rows) {
   108	    const c = classifyStatus(r.status);
   109	    if (!c.legal) { bad.push(`${r.id}: '${r.status}' is not in the ledger vocabulary`); continue; }
   110	    if (c.needsReason && !c.hasReason) { bad.push(`${r.id} is ${c.token} without a reason`); continue; }
   111	    if (c.isWaived && !waiveEvidence(flowText, r.id)) {
   112	      bad.push(`${r.id} is waived without a gates: entry recording the human decision`); continue;
   113	    }
   114	    if (c.token === 'open') { bad.push(`${r.id} is open`); continue; }
   115	    if (stage === 'archived' && !c.terminal) {
   116	      bad.push(c.token === 'fixed'
   117	        ? `${r.id} is fixed but never verified — reviewer must verify, or the human waives it`
   118	        : `${r.id} is rejected without reviewer concurrence — flip to rejected-verified, or the human waives it`);
   119	    }
   120	  }
   121	  return bad.length
   122	    ? { id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: bad.join('; ') }
   123	    : { id: 'C4', status: 'pass', detail: `${rows.length} row(s), none blocking` };
   124	}

   126	// the bundle review/ dir is the shared evidence root for C4 and C5 — when present it must be
   127	// a REAL contained directory (symlinked/escaping/non-dir entries block, never read through)
   128	function reviewDirDefect(dir) {
   129	  const rd = path.join(dir, 'review');
   130	  let st;
   131	  try { st = fs.lstatSync(rd); } catch { return null; }  // lstat, not exists: a dangling symlink is a defect, not absence
   132	  if (st.isSymbolicLink()) return `review is a symlink: ${rd}`;
   133	  if (!st.isDirectory()) return `review is not a directory: ${rd}`;
   134	  if (!containsReal(dir, rd)) return `review escapes the change dir: ${rd}`;
   135	  return null;
   136	}

   140	  const docs = [];

apriori/changes/archive-preflight/requirement/req-final.md:139:| **N2** | 解析结构与 resolver 对齐 | 凡是会让 `resolveChange()` 拒绝或选错的结构，同样拒绝并**采用 resolver 自己的判据**（见下方 §B2.1 的共享 predicate）。覆盖三类对象：<br>① **两个 trust root**：`<changes-dir>` 与 `<changes-dir>/archive` 的 `rootDefect`（含指向 changes 内部的 symlink、非目录、逃逸）；<br>② **待移动的 active 条目本身**（r4 补：`<changes-dir>/<name>` 可以是一个指向 changes root 内真实目录的 **symlink**——`discoverDeltas` 与 `archiveChangeDir` 的 containment 都放行它，move 会把它重命名成一个 **archived symlink**，而 `resolveChange()` 随后判定该 archived 条目为结构错误。因此必须用 resolver 的 **active-entry 判据**（lstat 优先、非 symlink、必须是目录、contained）先确认它）；<br>③ **同名归档条目**：stamp 形状合法的 symlink（无论排序早晚，resolver 都会 ERROR）、stamp 形状匹配但**日期非法**的目录（Gregorian 往返失败） |
apriori/changes/archive-preflight/requirement/req-final.md:140:| **N3** | 只对**会移动 bundle 的调用**求值 | 带 `--changes-dir` 的调用（含**带 `--changes-dir` 的 dry-run**，它预测同一次移动）执行 N0..N2；不带 `--changes-dir` 的调用**跳过**——它不创建归档目录，跑这组只会造出与实际写入无关的假阻断（r3·REQ-1-2） |
apriori/changes/archive-preflight/requirement/req-final.md:161:以及全部同名归档候选的判据（symlink / 非目录 / stamp 形状与 Gregorian 合法性 / containment）。
